The adverse condition is therefore both the cause and outcome of ineffective action.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Stitching, Strap, Patch, and Knot","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_43217dca4d00865c5d6b","text":"Flexible material is stitched, strapped, patched at a weak edge, and secured by a tight knot.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Inhabited and Deserted Places","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4371cd0099b147fbf803","text":"Inhabitation is a social quality of place, not merely occupancy. When people withdraw, familiarity becomes desolation and distance turns the abandoned settlement into a remote margin.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Decree, Upright Record, and Appraisal","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4588d7c986a470a8d685","text":"98:1, 98:3, 98:4, 98:6 (الكتاب/كتب); 98:3, 98:5 (قيمة/يقيموا/القيمة); 98:1, 98:4 (البينة)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Enrollment, Roster, and Payroll","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_458c328ff809ec25e605","text":"Workers are entered into a register, assigned service, and maintained by wage or stipend.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Round Basin, Star Cluster, and Calendar Rim","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_46bc18001601dff45308","text":"Objects become usable or comparable through proportion, bounded form, and load-bearing parts.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Security Held Beside Awe","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_4719d304510ba004544f","text":"Security and fear are not simple opposites here. Trust supplies a stable relation, while awe, alarm, and skittish recoil regulate approach to what carries authority or danger.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Estrus, Pairing, and Herd Separation","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_49914858787d48895434","text":"Pairing initiates the cycle, pregnancy changes the animal's social position, and delivery returns the scene to fertility and milk. Resolve is the point at which deliberation becomes commitment.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Inhabited and Deserted Places","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_66b6a38c20fbd667fe72","text":"98:1, 98:6 (أهل/كفروا); 98:8 (أبدا); 98:4 (بعد)","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Willing Gift and Welfare","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_66eafd03599cf6a73465","text":"A giver voluntarily presents a benefit in response to another's need.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"D:Outsider, Distant Kin, and Aversion","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_679a910f1dc181380cf0","text":"Distance is social before it is spatial: kin become remote, the settlement lies beyond familiar bounds, and the outsider evokes animal-like flight. The cluster gives exclusion a bodily image of recoil.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Penetrating Judgment and Total Commitment","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_68395f15834903cc2a5e","text":"A decision reaches through uncertainty and is carried out with the whole self.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Convalescence and Release from Illness","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_695453c22cb17b282717","text":"Recovery is expressed as separation: illness releases its hold and the patient exits it.
